Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a basics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 358. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 336/4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to basics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 358/336 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the basics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 860 and the convergence rate is O(195/n^5).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the basics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 860 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 195. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any basics module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 403. The projective resolution has length at most 284 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.
